29	    total: 0,
30	    filters: {},

33	
34	// Site-wide total of all published extensions across every category. Fetched
35	// once, independently of the active tab, so the heading number stays stable
36	// when the user flips between Recommended / Popular / Recent.
37	const { data: totalData } = await useFetch("/api/internal/extensions", {

42	    filters: {},
43	  }),
44	})
45	
46	const { data: facetsData } = await useFetch("/api/internal/facets", {
47	  key: "home-discovery-facets",

49	    creators: [],

52	  }),

55	const items = computed(() => gridData.value.items.slice(0, 12))

5	
6	type CategoryKey = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
7	
8	const CATEGORY_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [
9	  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills" },
10	  { key: "mcp", labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers" },
11	  { key: "slash", labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands" },
12	  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins" },
13	]
14	
15	const localeExtensionsPath = computed(() => localePath("/extensions"))
16	
17	function isCategoryActive(key: CategoryKey): boolean {
18	  if (route.path !== localeExtensionsPath.value) return false
19	  return route.query.category === key
20	}
21	</script>
22

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import { FUNC_CAT_COLORS, FUNC_TAXONOMY } from "~~/shared/taxonomy"
4	
5	const { t } = useI18n()
6	const route = useRoute()
7	const localePath = useLocalePath()
8	
9	const activeFuncCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.funcCat === "string" ? route.query.funcCat : null))
10	const activeSubCat = computed(() => (typeof route.query.subCat === "string" ? route.query.subCat : null))
11	const activeL2 = computed(() => (typeof route.query.l2 === "string" ? route.query.l2 : null))
12	
13	function buildHref(updates: Record<string, string | null>): string {
14	  const params = new URLSearchParams()
15	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
16	    if (typeof v === "string" && k !== "page") params.set(k, v)
17	  }
18	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(updates)) {
19	    if (v === null) params.delete(k)
20	    else params.set(k, v)
21	  }
22	  const qs = params.toString()
23	  const path = localePath("/extensions")
24	  return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
25	}
26	</script>
27	
28	<template>
29	  <div
30	    class="min-w-[200px] flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-3 text-(--color-ink) [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden"
31	  >
32	    <h2 class="px-2 pb-1.5 font-serif text-[12px] italic tracking-wide text-(--color-ink-muted)">
33	      {{ t("sidebar.categories") }}
34	    </h2>
35	    <div class="flex flex-col gap-px">
36	      <NuxtLink
37	        :to="buildHref({ funcCat: null, subCat: null, l2: null })"
38	        class="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[15px] font-semibold transition hover:bg-(--color-card)"
39	        :class="!activeFuncCat && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? 'text-(--color-ink)' : 'text-(--color-ink-muted)'"
40	      >
41	        <span class="bg-(--color-ink-muted) size-[9px] shrink-0 rounded-sm" />
42	        <span class="flex-1">{{ t("sidebar.allCategories") }}</span>
43	      </NuxtLink>
44	
45	      <div v-for="cat in FUNC_TAXONOMY" :key="cat.key">
46	        <NuxtLink
47	          :to="buildHref({ funcCat: activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? null : cat.key, subCat: null, l2: null })"
48	          class="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[15px] font-semibold transition hover:bg-(--color-card)"
49	          :class="activeFuncCat === cat.key && !activeSubCat && !activeL2 ? 'text-(--color-ink)' : 'text-(--color-ink-muted)'"
50	          :aria-expanded="activeFuncCat === cat.key"
51	        >
52	          <span class="size-[9px] shrink-0 rounded-sm" :style="{ background: FUNC_CAT_COLORS[cat.key] }" />
53	          <span class="flex-1">{{ t(`taxonomy.funcCat.${cat.key}`) }}</span>
54	          <ChevronDown v-if="activeFuncCat === cat.key" :size="14" class="shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)" aria-hidden="true" />
55	          <ChevronRight v-else :size="14" class="shrink-0 text-(--color-ink-muted)" aria-hidden="true" />
56	        </NuxtLink>
57	
58	        <div v-if="activeFuncCat === cat.key">
59	          <div v-for="l1 in cat.l1" :key="l1.key" class="ml-2">
60	            <NuxtLink
61	              :to="buildHref({ funcCat: cat.key, subCat: activeSubCat === l1.key ? null : l1.key, l2: null })"
62	              class="flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13.5px] font-medium hover:bg-(--color-card)"
63	              :class="activeSubCat === l1.key && !activeL2 ? 'text-(--color-ink) font-semibold' : 'text-(--color-ink)'"
64	              :aria-expanded="l1.l2.length > 0 ? activeSubCat === l1.key : undefined"

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Check, Copy } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{ command: string }>()
5	
6	const { t } = useI18n()
7	const copied = ref(false)
8	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
9	
10	async function copy() {
11	  if (!navigator.clipboard) return
12	  try {
13	    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.command)
14	    copied.value = true
15	    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
16	    timer = setTimeout(() => { copied.value = false }, 1400)
17	  } catch {
18	    // ignore — clipboard permissions vary
19	  }
20	}
21	
22	onBeforeUnmount(() => { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer) })
23	</script>
24	
25	<template>
26	  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) p-3 font-mono text-sm">
27	    <span class="flex-1 truncate">$ {{ command }}</span>
28	    <button
29	      type="button"
30	      class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs text-(--color-bg)/80 hover:text-(--color-bg)"
31	      :aria-label="copied ? t('extensions.copied') : t('extensions.copy')"
32	      @click="copy"
33	    >
34	      <component :is="copied ? Check : Copy" :size="12" aria-hidden="true" />
35	      {{ copied ? t("extensions.copied") : t("extensions.copy") }}
36	    </button>
37	  </div>
38	</template>
39

> AGENT

I've read the current home + layout surfaces (hero, discovery, topbar, sidebar, both layouts). A few candidate refinements stand out — but "refine" is open-ended, so I want to align on where to push before writing the final plan.

**What I see now:**
- Hero has two CTAs (`Browse skills` filled, `Install the CLI` outlined → anchors to a snippet visible right below). The second button is mostly decorative since the snippet is in-view.
- The page has ~4 paths to `/extensions` in one screen (hero CTA, discovery search submit, "Explore all skills" button, "View more"). Reads a little redundant.
- Eyebrow chip says `CURATED AI SKILLS`; discovery heading says `{count} extensions, **curated** for your team.` — "curated" repeats.
- `TopBar` active nav uses a tinted fill (`bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`), which conflicts with locked decision #11 ("Secondary active states ... use neutral ink + left-rule or bold weight, not tinted fills"). Sidebar already follows the rule; TopBar doesn't.
- `HomeTabs` is loud — `text-lg` semibold + `py-4` + 3px underline. Sits between two centered headlines (hero title and discovery heading) and competes.
- `HomeTagRail` mixes a filled "All" pill with outlined topic pills; reads as two systems.
- Hero gradient is light-theme-only; dark/mono get flat bg, so the hero band is much weaker in those themes.
- TopBar nav is `hidden md:flex` — mobile has no way to reach categories.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-continue-to-refine-gentle-moth.md",
  "content": "# Refine layout shell + home: cohesion pass\n\n## Context\n\nRecent merges (`refactor/layout-shell` #32, `refactor/home-refine` #33) shrunk the\nhero and reworked the layout shell. What's left is a coherence pass: the home is\nworking but has small duplications, inconsistencies with locked decision #11, and\nloud spots that compete with the actual product (the grid). The goal is *quiet\nthe surrounds, let the catalogue lead* — without redesigning anything.\n\nConcrete issues this pass addresses:\n\n1. **Hero/discovery overlap.** One screen offers 4 routes to `/extensions` (hero\n   \"Browse skills\" CTA, hero \"Install the CLI\" anchor, discovery search submit,\n   discovery \"Explore all skills\" button, \"View more\" link). The hero's\n   \"Install the CLI\" button anchors to a snippet that's already in view — pure\n   decoration.\n2. **\"Curated\" repeats.** Eyebrow chip says `CURATED AI SKILLS`; discovery\n   heading says `… curated for your team.` Same word, two adjacent blocks.\n3. **TopBar violates locked decision #11.** Active nav items use a tinted fill\n   (`bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`), but the locked rule says secondary active states\n   use *bold + rule*, not tinted fills. The sidebar already follows the rule;\n   the topbar drifts.\n4. **HomeTabs is loud.** `text-lg` semibold, `py-4`, 3px underline — visually\n   the heaviest element on the page, beating the grid it introduces.\n5. **Tag rail mixes systems.** \"All\" pill is filled (`bg-(--color-ink)/5`),\n   topic pills are outlined. Two pill systems in one row.\n6. **Hero is flat in dark/mono.** The light theme radial gradient is explicitly\n   overridden in dark/mono. With both hero and discovery using `--color-bg`,\n   the two bands run together in those themes.\n7. **Mobile has no category nav.** TopBar nav is `hidden md:flex`; under md the\n   user sees only the logo + utilities and has no link to `/extensions`.\n\n## Plan\n\n### 1. Tighten the hero — `app/components/home/HomeHero.vue`\n\n- **Remove** the outlined `Install the CLI` button (it anchors to the snippet\n  directly below — a non-action). Keep the install snippet inline; the section\n  still self-explains.\n- **Drop** the `#install-cli` anchor id on the snippet wrapper (no longer\n  needed).\n- **Simplify the eyebrow.** Replace `AGENTCENTER · CURATED AI SKILLS` with a\n  shorter, non-duplicating string. Proposed copy:\n  - EN: `THE AI EXTENSION REGISTRY`\n  - ZH: `AI 扩展注册中心`\n  Translation keys: `home.heroEyebrow` in `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`.\n- **Add a hairline separator below the hero** so it reads as a distinct band\n  in *all* themes: `border-b border-(--color-border)` on the section. ## Approved Plan:
# Refine layout shell + home: cohesion pass

## Context

Recent merges (`refactor/layout-shell` #32, `refactor/home-refine` #33) shrunk the
hero and reworked the layout shell. What's left is a coherence pass: the home is
working but has small duplications, inconsistencies with locked decision #11, and
loud spots that compete with the actual product (the grid). The goal is *quiet
the surrounds, let the catalogue lead* — without redesigning anything.

Concrete issues this pass addresses:

1. **Hero/discovery overlap.** One screen offers 4 routes to `/extensions` (hero
   "Browse skills" CTA, hero "Install the CLI" anchor, discovery search submit,
   discovery "Explore all skills" button, "View more" link). The hero's
   "Install the CLI" button anchors to a snippet that's already in view — pure
   decoration.
2. **"Curated" repeats.** Eyebrow chip says `CURATED AI SKILLS`; discovery
   heading says `… curated for your team.` Same word, two adjacent blocks.
3. **TopBar violates locked decision #11.** Active nav items use a tinted fill
   (`bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`), but the locked rule says secondary active states
   use *bold + rule*, not tinted fills. The sidebar already follows the rule;
   the topbar drifts.
4. **HomeTabs is loud.** `text-lg` semibold, `py-4`, 3px underline — visually
   the heaviest element on the page, beating the grid it introduces.
5. **Tag rail mixes systems.** "All" pill is filled (`bg-(--color-ink)/5`),
   topic pills are outlined. Two pill systems in one row.
6. **Hero is flat in dark/mono.** The light theme radial gradient is explicitly
   overridden in dark/mono. With both hero and discovery using `--color-bg`,
   the two bands run together in those themes.
7. **Mobile has no category nav.** TopBar nav is `hidden md:flex`; under md the
   user sees only the logo + utilities and has no link to `/extensions`.

## Plan

### 1. Tighten the hero — `app/components/home/HomeHero.vue`

- **Remove** the outlined `Install the CLI` button (it anchors to the snippet
  directly below — a non-action). Keep the install snippet inline; the section
  still self-explains.
- **Drop** the `#install-cli` anchor id on the snippet wrapper (no longer
  needed).
- **Simplify the eyebrow.** Replace `AGENTCENTER · CURATED AI SKILLS` with a
  shorter, non-duplicating string. Proposed copy:
  - EN: `THE AI EXTENSION REGISTRY`
  - ZH: `AI 扩展注册中心`
  Translation keys: `home.heroEyebrow` in `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`.
- **Add a hairline separator below the hero** so it reads as a distinct band
  in *all* themes: `border-b border-(--color-border)` on the section. In dark/
  mono this is now the only band cue; in light it crisps the gradient edge.
- **Dark/mono hero presence.** Inside the existing `:global(.dark) .home-hero,
  :global(.mono) .home-hero` rule, swap the flat `var(--color-bg)` for
  `var(--color-card)` so the hero is a hair lighter than the discovery band
  below it (matching the implicit lift the light-theme gradient provides).

### 2. Discovery heading — `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue`

- Update `home.discoveryHeading` to drop "curated":
  - EN: `"{count} extensions for your team."`
  - ZH: `"{count} 个扩展，为你的团队而备。"`
- `home.discoveryHeadingEmpty` likewise:
  - EN: `"AI extensions for your team."`
  - ZH: `"AI 扩展，为你的团队而备。"`

### 3. Quieten HomeTabs — `app/components/home/HomeTabs.vue`

Replace the trigger class on `TabsTrigger`:

```diff
- class="px-6 py-4 text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight
-        data-[state=active]:font-bold data-[state=active]:border-b-[3px]"
+ class="px-4 py-2.5 text-[15px] font-medium tracking-tight
+        text-(--color-ink-muted)
+        data-[state=active]:text-(--color-ink)
+        data-[state=active]:font-semibold
+        data-[state=active]:border-b-2"
```

Also reduce gap on the list: `gap-12` → `gap-8`. Keeps the centered row, but
the tabs no longer outweigh the grid.

### 4. Harmonize HomeTagRail — `app/components/home/HomeTagRail.vue`

Make the "All" pill share the topic-pill skeleton, differentiated by weight
only (matches decision #11's rule: bold weight, not tinted fill).

```diff
- class="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-(--color-ink)/25
-        bg-(--color-ink)/5 px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-(--color-ink)
-        transition hover:bg-(--color-ink)/10"
+ class="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-(--color-border)
+        px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-(--color-ink)
+        transition hover:border-(--color-ink)/30"
```

Topic pills stay as-is (`font-medium`, muted ink). The rail now reads as one
quiet system with "All" promoted by weight, not fill.

### 5. TopBar active state — `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`

Apply locked decision #11 to the inline nav. Replace the tinted-fill pattern
with **bold + bottom rule** (the horizontal analogue of the sidebar's left-
rule). Affects all 6 nav items (4 categories + Collections + Publish).

```diff
- class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-(--color-ink-muted)
-        hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)"
- :class="isCategoryActive(item.key)
-          ? 'text-(--color-ink) font-semibold bg-(--color-sidebar)/60'
-          : ''"
+ class="relative px-3 py-1.5 text-(--color-ink-muted)
+        hover:text-(--color-ink) transition-colors
+        after:absolute after:left-3 after:right-3 after:-bottom-px
+        after:h-px after:bg-transparent"
+ :class="isCategoryActive(item.key)
+          ? 'text-(--color-ink) font-semibold after:bg-(--color-ink)'
+          : ''"
```

Same treatment for the two `active-class` links (Collections, Publish).
The `relative` + pseudo-element keeps the rule precisely under the label
without shifting layout. No more sidebar-tinted fill.

### 6. Mobile nav affordance — `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`

Minimal-footprint fix: add a single visible link that surfaces below `md`.
Don't introduce a hamburger sheet for one link; the discovery search on the
home is already a strong "find things" affordance.

After the logo, before the existing `hidden md:flex` nav:

```vue
<NuxtLink
  :to="{ path: localeExtensionsPath, query: { category: 'skills' } }"
  class="md:hidden text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)"
>
  {{ t("nav.browse") }}
</NuxtLink>
```

Add translation key `nav.browse`:
- EN: `"Browse"`
- ZH: `"浏览"`

### 7. Existing component tests to update

- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — if there is a `TopBar.nuxt.test.ts`
  (none in the current `app/components/layout` listing — only `Sidebar`,
  `LocaleSwitch`, `ThemeSwitch`), skip. Otherwise, sanity-check active-state
  class assertions.
- `app/components/layout/Sidebar.nuxt.test.ts`, `LocaleSwitch.nuxt.test.ts`,
  `ThemeSwitch.nuxt.test.ts` — unaffected.
- Home components have no `.nuxt.test.ts` files at present; visual changes
  are caught at PR review time.

## Critical files

- `app/components/home/HomeHero.vue` — drop CLI button, simplify eyebrow,
  add bottom rule, lift dark/mono bg
- `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue` — heading copy via i18n keys only
- `app/components/home/HomeTabs.vue` — quieter trigger styling
- `app/components/home/HomeTagRail.vue` — unify "All" pill
- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — active-state rewrite + mobile Browse link
- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — `home.heroEyebrow`,
  `home.discoveryHeading`, `home.discoveryHeadingEmpty`, `nav.browse`

## Reused primitives (not changed)

- `InstallCommand` (`app/components/extension/InstallCommand.vue`) — still
  rendered inside the hero, unchanged.
- `Tabs`, `TabsList`, `TabsTrigger` shadcn-vue primitives — only class strings
  on `TabsTrigger` change.
- `Button`, `Input`, `Label` in `HomeDiscovery` — untouched.

## Out of scope (intentionally)

- No layout-tree changes (`default.vue` / `browse.vue` left as-is).
- No grid (`ExtGrid`) or card changes.
- No sidebar changes (already conformant with decision #11).
- No new components.

## Verification

1. `bun run dev` and open `/en` and `/zh`:
   - Hero: only one CTA button (`Browse skills`), install snippet still
     visible inline below it. Eyebrow is the new short string.
   - Hero has a visible hairline bottom border separating it from the
     discovery section in all three themes.
   - Discovery heading reads "{N} extensions for your team." with no
     "curated" repetition.
2. Theme toggle through Editorial Ivory / Dark / Mono Clean:
   - Light keeps the radial gradient.
   - Dark + Mono show the hero on `--color-card`, visibly distinct from the
     discovery band below.
3. TopBar: hover and active states on `Skills`, `MCP Servers`, `Slash`,
   `Plugins`, `Collections`, `Publish`. Active = bold + a single ink-colored
   2px-ish bottom rule under the label. No tinted fill anywhere.
4. Resize to `<768px`: a single "Browse" link is visible next to the logo;
   the desktop nav is hidden as before. Click navigates to
   `/{locale}/extensions?category=skills`.
5. HomeTabs: the three tabs (Recommended / Popular / New) are visibly lighter
   than before — `text-[15px]`, medium weight, thin underline on active.
6. HomeTagRail: scan the row — every chip uses the same outlined skeleton;
   "All" stands out only by weight.
7. `bun run validate` (or whatever the repo's lint/typecheck script is).
   - Spot check: no unused imports left in `HomeHero.vue` after removing the
     `Terminal` icon button (the `Terminal` lucide import should be removed).
8. Run existing tests: `bun test` — `Sidebar`, `LocaleSwitch`, `ThemeSwitch`
   suites should still pass; no changes to their components.

## Commit shape (per CLAUDE.md §Workflow)

Per the project's "commit per coherent unit" rule, expect ~3 commits on a
single feature branch, e.g.:

- `refactor(home): tighten hero and discovery copy`
- `refactor(home): quieten tabs and harmonize tag rail`
- `refactor(topbar): align active state with decision #11, add mobile Browse`

All under one PR with conventional `refactor(home|topbar): …` titles. No
agent-attribution trailer per project convention.

```json
{
  "command": "git add app/components/layout/TopBar.vue && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nrefactor(topbar): replace tinted active fill with bold + bottom rule\n\nLocked decision #11 says secondary active states use bold weight or a rule,\nnot tinted fills. The sidebar already follows the rule; the topbar drifted\nto `bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`. Switch the inline nav (4 category links +\nCollections + Publish) to the horizontal analogue: bold weight + a 1px\nink-colored after-pseudo rule under the label. The rule is positioned via\n`absolute inset-x-3 -bottom-px` so it tracks the link's text without\nshifting layout.\n\nAlso surface a single mobile Browse link next to the logo so users below\nthe md breakpoint have a way into the catalogue — the full category nav\nstays desktop-only, no hamburger sheet.\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit TopBar refinement"
}
```

- `HomeDiscovery` copy — removed the "curated" echo from `discoveryHeading` and `discoveryHeadingEmpty` in both locales.
- `HomeTabs.vue` — `text-lg` → `text-[15px]`, `py-4` → `py-2.5`, 3px → 2px underline, muted ink resting + full ink on active, gap-12 → gap-8.
- `HomeTagRail.vue` — "All" pill now matches topic-pill skeleton (border, no fill), differentiated only by weight per decision #11.
- `TopBar.vue` — active state moved from tinted fill to bold + 1px bottom rule (decision #11 compliance), and a mobile-only "Browse" link surfaces next to the logo under `md`.
- `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json` — copy updates + new `nav.browse` key + removed unused `home.heroCtaCli`.

`bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run test` (242/242) all pass. Nothing pushed.

1	# Refine layout shell + home: cohesion pass
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	Recent merges (`refactor/layout-shell` #32, `refactor/home-refine` #33) shrunk the
6	hero and reworked the layout shell. What's left is a coherence pass: the home is
7	working but has small duplications, inconsistencies with locked decision #11, and
8	loud spots that compete with the actual product (the grid). The goal is *quiet
9	the surrounds, let the catalogue lead* — without redesigning anything.
10	
11	Concrete issues this pass addresses:
12	
13	1. **Hero/discovery overlap.** One screen offers 4 routes to `/extensions` (hero
14	   "Browse skills" CTA, hero "Install the CLI" anchor, discovery search submit,
15	   discovery "Explore all skills" button, "View more" link). The hero's
16	   "Install the CLI" button anchors to a snippet that's already in view — pure
17	   decoration.
18	2. **"Curated" repeats.** Eyebrow chip says `CURATED AI SKILLS`; discovery
19	   heading says `… curated for your team.` Same word, two adjacent blocks.
20	3. **TopBar violates locked decision #11.** Active nav items use a tinted fill
21	   (`bg-(--color-sidebar)/60`), but the locked rule says secondary active states
22	   use *bold + rule*, not tinted fills. The sidebar already follows the rule;
23	   the topbar drifts.
24	4. **HomeTabs is loud.** `text-lg` semibold, `py-4`, 3px underline — visually
25	   the heaviest element on the page, beating the grid it introduces.
26	5. **Tag rail mixes systems.** "All" pill is filled (`bg-(--color-ink)/5`),
27	   topic pills are outlined. Two pill systems in one row.
28	6. **Hero is flat in dark/mono.** The light theme radial gradient is explicitly
29	   overridden in dark/mono. With both hero and discovery using `--color-bg`,
30	   the two bands run together in those themes.
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-continue-to-refine-gentle-moth.md",
  "content": "# Restructure TopBar nav: Trending, More, Publish-as-icon\n\n## Context\n\nThe previous cohesion pass (commits `f49567a`, `0ac4006`, `fd154f8` on\n`refactor/home-cohesion-pass`) is shipped. The TopBar is now visually\ncorrect (decision #11), but its information architecture still treats all\nsix entries as peers — `Skills · MCP Servers · Slash Commands · Plugins ·\nCollections · Publish`. Two problems:\n\n1. **No way back to \"what's hot\" from non-home pages.** Once a user has\n   drilled into `/extensions` or a detail page, there's no nav shortcut to\n   the curated home discovery view. We want a `Trending` entry that lands\n   on the home discovery section (Recommended tab is the default there).\n2. **Long tail of categories crowds the nav.** Slash Commands and Plugins\n   are smaller catalogues than Skills and MCP. Demote them under a `More`\n   dropdown so the primary row stays three discovery items + Collections.\n3. **Publish is a write action sitting in an explore row.** Move it to the\n   right group as an `Upload`-icon link next to Theme / Locale / User —\n   the iconographic peers it actually belongs with.\n\nNet result, desktop:\n\n```\n[Logo] Trending  Skills  MCP Servers  More ▾  Collections        [↥ Publish] [☼ Theme] [文/A Locale] [User]\n```\n\nMobile is unchanged (logo + the single `Browse` link from the prior pass).\n\n## Plan\n\n### 1. Home anchor target — `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue`\n\nGive the discovery `<section>` an id so `Trending` can scroll to it from\nany route. The sticky 64px header would clip the heading otherwise, so\nalso set `scroll-margin-top` to compensate.\n\n```diff\n- <section class=\"mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-12 sm:py-16\">\n+ <section\n+   id=\"discovery\"\n+   class=\"mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-12 sm:py-16 scroll-mt-16\"\n+ >\n```\n\n`activeTab` in `HomeDiscovery.vue` already defaults to `\"official\"`\n(Recommended), so an anchor jump lands on the Recommended view with no\nextra wiring.\n\n### 2. TopBar restructure — `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`\n\n#### 2a. Split the category list\n\nCurrently one flat `CATEGORY_ITEMS` array drives the inline nav. Split it\ninto a *primary* set (rendered inline) and a *more* set (rendered inside\nthe dropdown).\n\n```ts\ntype CategoryKey = \"skills\" | \"mcp\" | \"slash\" | \"plugins\"\n\nconst PRIMARY_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [\n  { key: \"skills\", labelKey: \"sidebar.skills\" },\n  { key: \"mcp\",    labelKey: \"sidebar.mcpServers\" },\n]\n\nconst MORE_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [\n  { key: \"slash\",   labelKey: \"sidebar.slashCommands\" },\n  { key: \"plugins\", labelKey: \"sidebar.plugins\" },\n]\n```\n\n#### 2b. Insert `Trending` at the head of the nav\n\nAnchor to home discovery; locale-prefixed via `useLocalePath`. No active\nstate — it's a navigation shortcut, not a section indicator.\n\n```vue\n<NuxtLink\n  :to=\"localePath({ path: '/', hash: '#discovery' })\"\n  class=\"relative px-3 py-1.5 text-(--color-ink-muted)\n         transition-colors hover:text-(--color-ink)\"\n>\n  {{ t(\"nav.trending\") }}\n</NuxtLink>\n```\n\n#### 2c. `More ▾` dropdown via existing `Popover` primitive\n\nUse the same `Popover` + `PopoverTrigger` + `PopoverContent` pattern\nalready established in `app/components/layout/ThemeSwitch.vue`. No new\nprimitive; no `DropdownMenu` introduction.\n\n- Trigger: a button styled to match the inline nav links exactly (same\n  padding, muted ink, hover ink, bold + after-rule when *any* of the More\n  items is the active route).\n- Content: a 180–200px panel with a small inner padding (matches\n  ThemeSwitch sizing), two NuxtLinks to `/extensions?category=slash` and\n  `/extensions?category=plugins`, each with a left-rule active state\n  (mirrors the sidebar treatment so the dropdown reads as a mini-sidebar).\n- Caret: `ChevronDown` from `lucide-vue-next`, 14px, muted ink.\n\nActive detection helper:\n\n```ts\nconst isMoreActive = computed(() =>\n  MORE_ITEMS.some(it => isCategoryActive(it.key)),\n)\n```\n\n#### 2d. Demote `Publish` to a right-side icon\n\nRemove the `Publish` link from the inline nav. Add it on the right side,\n**before** `ThemeSwitch`, sized and styled to match the existing utility\nbuttons (32px circle, transparent border, hover bg, focus ring) — see\nThemeSwitch trigger lines 37–42 as the reference shape.\n\n```vue\n<NuxtLink\n  :to=\"localePath('/publish')\"\n  class=\"hidden md:inline-flex size-8 rounded-full items-center justify-center\n         border border-transparent text-(--color-ink-muted)\n         hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) hover:text-(--color-ink)\n         focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2\n         focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)\"\n  :aria-label=\"t('nav.publish')\"\n  :title=\"t('nav.publish')\"\n  active-class=\"text-(--color-ink)\"\n>\n  <Upload :size=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n</NuxtLink>\n```\n\n`active-class` flips the icon to full ink when on `/publish`. No bottom\nrule — the right-side utilities use color-only active states (matches\nThemeSwitch's `data-[state=open]:border-(--color-border)` pattern, though\nwe don't need an open state for a plain link).\n\nImports to add: `import { ChevronDown, Upload } from \"lucide-vue-next\"`.\n\n#### 2e. Mobile\n\nThe existing `md:hidden` Browse link stays. The new Trending / More /\nCollections nav and the new Publish icon all live inside the existing\n`hidden md:flex` nav (or are explicitly `hidden md:inline-flex` in the\nPublish icon's case). Mobile bar remains: `Logo · Browse · Theme · Locale ·\nUser` — unchanged.\n\n### 3. i18n — `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`\n\nAdd two new keys under `nav`. The slash/plugins menu items reuse the\nexisting `sidebar.slashCommands` and `sidebar.plugins` translations\n(already present and correct in both locales).\n\n```diff\n   \"nav\": {\n     ...\n     \"userMenu\": \"Account menu\",\n     \"comingSoon\": \"Coming soon\",\n-    \"browse\": \"Browse\"\n+    \"browse\": \"Browse\",\n+    \"trending\": \"Trending\",\n+    \"more\": \"More\"\n   },\n```\n\n```diff\n   \"nav\": {\n     ...\n     \"userMenu\": \"账号菜单\",\n     \"comingSoon\": \"敬请期待\",\n-    \"browse\": \"浏览\"\n+    \"browse\": \"浏览\",\n+    \"trending\": \"热榜\",\n+    \"more\": \"更多\"\n   },\n```\n\n### 4. Tests\n\n- No existing `TopBar.nuxt.test.ts`. Skip — visual changes covered by PR\n  review.\n- `app/components/layout/{Sidebar,LocaleSwitch,ThemeSwitch}.nuxt.test.ts`\n  are unaffected; they don't import `TopBar`.\n\n## Critical files\n\n- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — primary work: split items, add\n  Trending link, add More popover, move Publish to right group, add\n  `Upload` + `ChevronDown` imports.\n- `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue` — single line: add `id` and\n  `scroll-mt-16` on the section.\n- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — `nav.trending` +\n  `nav.more` only.\n\n## Reused primitives (not changed)\n\n- `Popover`, `PopoverTrigger`, `PopoverContent` from `app/components/ui/`\n  (same trio used by `ThemeSwitch.vue` and `SaveToCollectionPopover.vue`).\n- `useLocalePath`, `useI18n`, `useRoute` (all already used in current\n  `TopBar.vue`).\n- Lucide icons via `lucide-vue-next` (already a project dep).\n\n## Out of scope (intentionally)\n\n- No new `DropdownMenu` shadcn-vue component (Popover suffices).\n- No changes to the `Sidebar`, `HomeHero`, `HomeTabs`, `HomeTagRail`, or\n  any extension/detail components.\n- No mobile sheet/hamburger redesign.\n- No new routes — `Trending` is just `/#discovery`.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `bun run dev`. From `/en` (any non-home page, e.g. `/en/extensions`):\n   - Click `Trending` — URL changes to `/en/#discovery`, page scrolls to\n     the discovery section with the heading visible (not clipped under\n     the sticky header).\n   - Repeat from `/en/cli` and `/en/collections` to confirm cross-route\n     behavior.\n2. Click `More ▾`:\n   - Popover opens below the trigger with two items: Slash Commands and\n     Plugins, both linking to `/extensions?category=…`.\n   - Navigating to either category closes the popover and lands on the\n     filtered grid.\n   - When already on a More category, the `More` trigger shows the same\n     bold + bottom-rule active treatment as the primary nav items.\n3. Hover and click the `Upload` icon in the right group:\n   - Tooltip / `aria-label` reads \"Publish\" (zh: \"发布\").\n   - Click navigates to `/{locale}/publish`. While on `/publish`, the icon\n     renders in full ink (otherwise muted).\n4. Resize <768px:\n   - Trending / More / Collections / Publish-icon all disappear.\n   - The `Browse` link from the previous pass remains visible.\n5. Theme toggle through Ivory / Dark / Mono — Popover + icon read\n   correctly in all three.\n6. Keyboard: tab through TopBar — order is Logo → Browse (mobile only) →\n   Trending → Skills → MCP Servers → More trigger → Collections →\n   Publish icon → Theme → Locale → User. Popover opens on Enter/Space and\n   closes on Escape (Reka UI default).\n7. Repeat the same checks from `/zh` to confirm copy: 热榜 / 更多 / 发布.\n8. `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test` all green.\n\n## Commit shape (per CLAUDE.md §Workflow)\n\nOne feature branch (`refactor/topbar-restructure` or similar), expect ~2\ncommits:\n\n- `feat(topbar): add Trending shortcut and collapse Slash/Plugins under More`\n- `refactor(topbar): demote Publish to a right-side Upload icon`\n\nCould also be a single `refactor(topbar): restructure nav into primary/\nmore/publish-icon` if the diff is tight. No agent-attribution trailer per\nproject convention.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Restructure TopBar nav: Trending, More, Publish-as-icon

## Context

The previous cohesion pass (commits `f49567a`, `0ac4006`, `fd154f8` on
`refactor/home-cohesion-pass`) is shipped. The TopBar is now visually
correct (decision #11), but its information architecture still treats all
six entries as peers — `Skills · MCP Servers · Slash Commands · Plugins ·
Collections · Publish`. Two problems:

1. **No way back to "what's hot" from non-home pages.** Once a user has
   drilled into `/extensions` or a detail page, there's no nav shortcut to
   the curated home discovery view. We want a `Trending` entry that lands
   on the home discovery section (Recommended tab is the default there).
2. **Long tail of categories crowds the nav.** Slash Commands and Plugins
   are smaller catalogues than Skills and MCP. Demote them under a `More`
   dropdown so the primary row stays three discovery items + Collections.
3. **Publish is a write action sitting in an explore row.** Move it to the
   right group as an `Upload`-icon link next to Theme / Locale / User —
   the iconographic peers it actually belongs with.

Net result, desktop:

```
[Logo] Trending  Skills  MCP Servers  More ▾  Collections        [↥ Publish] [☼ Theme] [文/A Locale] [User]
```

Mobile is unchanged (logo + the single `Browse` link from the prior pass).

## Plan

### 1. Home anchor target — `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue`

Give the discovery `<section>` an id so `Trending` can scroll to it from
any route. The sticky 64px header would clip the heading otherwise, so
also set `scroll-margin-top` to compensate.

```diff
- <section class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-12 sm:py-16">
+ <section
+   id="discovery"
+   class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-12 sm:py-16 scroll-mt-16"
+ >
```

`activeTab` in `HomeDiscovery.vue` already defaults to `"official"`
(Recommended), so an anchor jump lands on the Recommended view with no
extra wiring.

### 2. TopBar restructure — `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue`

#### 2a. Split the category list

Currently one flat `CATEGORY_ITEMS` array drives the inline nav. Split it
into a *primary* set (rendered inline) and a *more* set (rendered inside
the dropdown).

```ts
type CategoryKey = "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"

const PRIMARY_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [
  { key: "skills", labelKey: "sidebar.skills" },
  { key: "mcp",    labelKey: "sidebar.mcpServers" },
]

const MORE_ITEMS: { key: CategoryKey; labelKey: string }[] = [
  { key: "slash",   labelKey: "sidebar.slashCommands" },
  { key: "plugins", labelKey: "sidebar.plugins" },
]
```

#### 2b. Insert `Trending` at the head of the nav

Anchor to home discovery; locale-prefixed via `useLocalePath`. No active
state — it's a navigation shortcut, not a section indicator.

```vue
<NuxtLink
  :to="localePath({ path: '/', hash: '#discovery' })"
  class="relative px-3 py-1.5 text-(--color-ink-muted)
         transition-colors hover:text-(--color-ink)"
>
  {{ t("nav.trending") }}
</NuxtLink>
```

#### 2c. `More ▾` dropdown via existing `Popover` primitive

Use the same `Popover` + `PopoverTrigger` + `PopoverContent` pattern
already established in `app/components/layout/ThemeSwitch.vue`. No new
primitive; no `DropdownMenu` introduction.

- Trigger: a button styled to match the inline nav links exactly (same
  padding, muted ink, hover ink, bold + after-rule when *any* of the More
  items is the active route).
- Content: a 180–200px panel with a small inner padding (matches
  ThemeSwitch sizing), two NuxtLinks to `/extensions?category=slash` and
  `/extensions?category=plugins`, each with a left-rule active state
  (mirrors the sidebar treatment so the dropdown reads as a mini-sidebar).
- Caret: `ChevronDown` from `lucide-vue-next`, 14px, muted ink.

Active detection helper:

```ts
const isMoreActive = computed(() =>
  MORE_ITEMS.some(it => isCategoryActive(it.key)),
)
```

#### 2d. Demote `Publish` to a right-side icon

Remove the `Publish` link from the inline nav. Add it on the right side,
**before** `ThemeSwitch`, sized and styled to match the existing utility
buttons (32px circle, transparent border, hover bg, focus ring) — see
ThemeSwitch trigger lines 37–42 as the reference shape.

```vue
<NuxtLink
  :to="localePath('/publish')"
  class="hidden md:inline-flex size-8 rounded-full items-center justify-center
         border border-transparent text-(--color-ink-muted)
         hover:bg-(--color-sidebar) hover:text-(--color-ink)
         focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2
         focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)"
  :aria-label="t('nav.publish')"
  :title="t('nav.publish')"
  active-class="text-(--color-ink)"
>
  <Upload :size="16" aria-hidden="true" />
</NuxtLink>
```

`active-class` flips the icon to full ink when on `/publish`. No bottom
rule — the right-side utilities use color-only active states (matches
ThemeSwitch's `data-[state=open]:border-(--color-border)` pattern, though
we don't need an open state for a plain link).

Imports to add: `import { ChevronDown, Upload } from "lucide-vue-next"`.

#### 2e. Mobile

The existing `md:hidden` Browse link stays. The new Trending / More /
Collections nav and the new Publish icon all live inside the existing
`hidden md:flex` nav (or are explicitly `hidden md:inline-flex` in the
Publish icon's case). Mobile bar remains: `Logo · Browse · Theme · Locale ·
User` — unchanged.

### 3. i18n — `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`

Add two new keys under `nav`. The slash/plugins menu items reuse the
existing `sidebar.slashCommands` and `sidebar.plugins` translations
(already present and correct in both locales).

```diff
   "nav": {
     ...
     "userMenu": "Account menu",
     "comingSoon": "Coming soon",
-    "browse": "Browse"
+    "browse": "Browse",
+    "trending": "Trending",
+    "more": "More"
   },
```

```diff
   "nav": {
     ...
     "userMenu": "账号菜单",
     "comingSoon": "敬请期待",
-    "browse": "浏览"
+    "browse": "浏览",
+    "trending": "热榜",
+    "more": "更多"
   },
```

### 4. Tests

- No existing `TopBar.nuxt.test.ts`. Skip — visual changes covered by PR
  review.
- `app/components/layout/{Sidebar,LocaleSwitch,ThemeSwitch}.nuxt.test.ts`
  are unaffected; they don't import `TopBar`.

## Critical files

- `app/components/layout/TopBar.vue` — primary work: split items, add
  Trending link, add More popover, move Publish to right group, add
  `Upload` + `ChevronDown` imports.
- `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue` — single line: add `id` and
  `scroll-mt-16` on the section.
- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — `nav.trending` +
  `nav.more` only.

## Reused primitives (not changed)

- `Popover`, `PopoverTrigger`, `PopoverContent` from `app/components/ui/`
  (same trio used by `ThemeSwitch.vue` and `SaveToCollectionPopover.vue`).
- `useLocalePath`, `useI18n`, `useRoute` (all already used in current
  `TopBar.vue`).
- Lucide icons via `lucide-vue-next` (already a project dep).

## Out of scope (intentionally)

- No new `DropdownMenu` shadcn-vue component (Popover suffices).
- No changes to the `Sidebar`, `HomeHero`, `HomeTabs`, `HomeTagRail`, or
  any extension/detail components.
- No mobile sheet/hamburger redesign.
- No new routes — `Trending` is just `/#discovery`.

## Verification

1. `bun run dev`. From `/en` (any non-home page, e.g. `/en/extensions`):
   - Click `Trending` — URL changes to `/en/#discovery`, page scrolls to
     the discovery section with the heading visible (not clipped under
     the sticky header).
   - Repeat from `/en/cli` and `/en/collections` to confirm cross-route
     behavior.
2. Click `More ▾`:
   - Popover opens below the trigger with two items: Slash Commands and
     Plugins, both linking to `/extensions?category=…`.
   - Navigating to either category closes the popover and lands on the
     filtered grid.
   - When already on a More category, the `More` trigger shows the same
     bold + bottom-rule active treatment as the primary nav items.
3. Hover and click the `Upload` icon in the right group:
   - Tooltip / `aria-label` reads "Publish" (zh: "发布").
   - Click navigates to `/{locale}/publish`. While on `/publish`, the icon
     renders in full ink (otherwise muted).
4. Resize <768px:
   - Trending / More / Collections / Publish-icon all disappear.
   - The `Browse` link from the previous pass remains visible.
5. Theme toggle through Ivory / Dark / Mono — Popover + icon read
   correctly in all three.
6. Keyboard: tab through TopBar — order is Logo → Browse (mobile only) →
   Trending → Skills → MCP Servers → More trigger → Collections →
   Publish icon → Theme → Locale → User. Popover opens on Enter/Space and
   closes on Escape (Reka UI default).
7. Repeat the same checks from `/zh` to confirm copy: 热榜 / 更多 / 发布.
8. `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test` all green.

## Commit shape (per CLAUDE.md §Workflow)

One feature branch (`refactor/topbar-restructure` or similar), expect ~2
commits:

- `feat(topbar): add Trending shortcut and collapse Slash/Plugins under More`
- `refactor(topbar): demote Publish to a right-side Upload icon`

Could also be a single `refactor(topbar): restructure nav into primary/
more/publish-icon` if the diff is tight. No agent-attribution trailer per
project convention.

```

> AGENT

Branch `refactor/home-cohesion-pass` now has 4 commits — the prior cohesion pass (3) plus the new TopBar restructure (1).

**TopBar restructure summary:**
- **Trending** at the head of the nav → `/{locale}/#discovery` (lands on the default Recommended tab in `HomeDiscovery`, with `scroll-mt-16` so the sticky header doesn't clip it).
- **Slash Commands + Plugins** collapsed into a **More ▾** dropdown using the existing `Popover` primitive (no new shadcn-vue component). Active state on the trigger when any of its items matches the current category; sidebar-style left-rule on the items themselves.
- **Publish** moved from an inline explore link to a right-side **Upload** icon next to ThemeSwitch — color-only active state to match its iconographic peers.
- New i18n keys: `nav.trending` (Trending / 热榜), `nav.more` (More / 更多).

Mobile is untouched: logo + Browse link only.

`bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run test` (242/242) all green. Nothing pushed.

> DEVELOPER

is it better to put more at the last? what do you think?

> AGENT

Yes — convention is on your side: residual "More" menus almost always trail (iOS tab bars, Office ribbon, GitHub's overflow nav). The main tradeoff is that putting it after Collections breaks the implicit "all categories sit together" grouping, but I think the convention win outweighs that — `Trending | Skills | MCP Servers | Collections | More ▾` reads as "primary nav, plus overflow" which is what we actually mean. Want me to reorder it?

> DEVELOPER

yes, reorder it

> AGENT

I have a clean read on the trade-offs. One thing I want to pin down before writing the plan — "3 lines" is viewport-dependent (the grid is `auto-fill` 320px-min), so it lands differently at common widths:

| count | desktop (3 col) | tablet (2 col) | mobile (1 col) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 9 | 3 lines | 4.5 ← half row | 9 |
| 12 (current) | 4 lines | 6 | 12 |
| 18 | 6 lines | 9 | 18 |
| 24 | 8 lines | 12 | 24 |

Since you said "show *more*", 9 is out (fewer than today). Picking one that gives full rows on both 3-col and 2-col widths matters.

## Approved Plan:
# Discovery section: skills-aware heading + populate Recommended tab

## Context

The home discovery section currently shows three tabs (Recommended /
Popular / New), all of which hard-code `category: "skills"` in their
fetch — so the grid is skills-only. Two mismatches result:

1. **Heading lies.** The headline reads `{count} extensions for your
   team.`, but the count is a *cross-category* total (skills + MCP +
   slash + plugins) while the grid below only renders skills. The user
   asked for the heading to say "skills" — and we should also filter the
   `totalData` fetch to `category=skills` so the number matches reality.
2. **Recommended is sparse.** It shows a single row of 3 cards. The
   filter is `eq(extensions.badge, "official")` (`shared/search/query.ts:58-60`),
   and only **2** catalog entries currently have `badge: "official"`:
   `sql-tuner` and `aws-iam-policy-helper`. The slice limit (12) and API
   page size (24) are not the bottleneck — content is. The other tabs
   (Popular, New) are unfiltered so they already fill several rows.

User decision (this session): flag more catalog skills as official so
Recommended fills out naturally, rather than redefining the tab or
bolting on a fallback.

## Plan

### 1. Heading copy + scope — `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue`

Filter `totalData` to skills, and update the i18n strings. The grid-data
fetch already filters to skills, so we just align the headline.

```diff
 const { data: totalData } = await useFetch("/api/internal/extensions", {
   key: "home-discovery-total",
+  query: { category: "skills" },
   default: () => ({
     items: [] as ExtensionListItem[],
     total: 0,
     filters: {},
   }),
 })
```

### 2. i18n — `i18n/locales/{en,zh}.json`

```diff
- "discoveryHeading": "{count} extensions for your team.",
- "discoveryHeadingEmpty": "AI extensions for your team.",
+ "discoveryHeading": "{count} skills for your team.",
+ "discoveryHeadingEmpty": "AI skills for your team.",
```

```diff
- "discoveryHeading": "{count} 个扩展，为你的团队而备。",
- "discoveryHeadingEmpty": "AI 扩展，为你的团队而备。",
+ "discoveryHeading": "{count} 个技能，为你的团队而备。",
+ "discoveryHeadingEmpty": "AI 技能，为你的团队而备。",
```

### 3. Promote 11 catalog skills to `badge: "official"` — `shared/data/catalog.ts`

Editorial picks chosen for: ≥4.5★, healthy downloads, and a balanced
spread across `funcCat` × `subCat` cells so Recommended reads as a real
curation rather than "popular sorted differently". Skips entries already
flagged `popular` (those represent a separate badge axis).

End state: **13 official skills** (current 2 + 11 new), enough to fill
~4 rows on a 3-col desktop grid (12 cards on screen) with one spare.

| slug | funcCat / subCat | dl | ★ | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sql-tuner` *(keep)* | workTask / softDev | 142.5k | 4.9 | already official |
| `aws-iam-policy-helper` *(keep)* | business / cloud | 102.3k | 4.8 | already official |
| `api-spec-helper` | workTask / systemDesign | 52.8k | 4.6 | OpenAPI/REST helper, broadly useful |
| `dockerfile-linter` | workTask / softDev | 89.3k | 4.7 | container hygiene, high signal |
| `react-hook-genie` | workTask / softDev | 72.4k | 4.7 | frontend mainline |
| `vitest-case-writer` | workTask / testing | 56.7k | 4.7 | top of testing subcat |
| `postman-helper` | business / network | 47.2k | 4.5 | best-of-class for network |
| `k8s-manifest-gen` | business / cloud | 78.5k | 4.7 | second cloud pick alongside IAM |
| `readme-generator` | tools / docs | 76.3k | 4.7 | universal team doc |
| `regex-builder` | tools / data | 51.2k | 4.7 | high-utility data tool |
| `conventional-commit-helper` | tools / vcs | 89.1k | 4.7 | strong vcs default |
| `github-actions-helper` | tools / vcs | 61.8k | 4.7 | second vcs pick |
| `test-this` | workTask / testing | 56.2k | 4.7 | filler-zone but quality-fit |

Spread: workTask 6 / business 3 / tools 4. Sub-balance: softDev 3,
testing 2, vcs 2, cloud 2, systemDesign 1, network 1, docs 1, data 1.

Edit pattern per entry — insert `badge: "official",` immediately before
`downloadsCount:`, matching the existing 2 entries' shape (see lines
231-233 and 639-641).

The seed script `scripts/seed-catalog.ts` upserts these entries on every
`vercel-build`, so the database picks up the badges on the next deploy.
For local dev: re-run `bun run scripts/seed-catalog.ts` (or hit
`db:seed:catalog` if a script alias exists).

### 4. (Out of scope this pass)

- No change to the slice limit (`items.slice(0, 12)`) — 12 is fine; the
  earlier "show more" perception came from sparse data, not the cap.
- No tab semantic changes.
- No new component or layout work.
- No changes to Popular / New tabs (already populated).

## Critical files

- `app/components/home/HomeDiscovery.vue` — add `query: { category: "skills" }`
  to the `home-discovery-total` `useFetch`.
- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — `discoveryHeading` +
  `discoveryHeadingEmpty` copy.
- `shared/data/catalog.ts` — add `badge: "official"` to 11 entries
  (slugs listed above).

## Reused mechanisms

- `badge: "official"` field on `CatalogEntry` (already in the schema and
  consumed by the seed at `scripts/seed-catalog.ts`).
- `filter === "official"` predicate in `shared/search/query.ts:58-60`,
  unchanged.
- `useFetch` keying in `HomeDiscovery.vue` — only the query payload of
  `home-discovery-total` changes; the cache key stays stable.

## Verification

1. Re-seed the local DB so the new badges apply:

   ```sh
   bun run scripts/seed-catalog.ts
   ```

2. `bun run dev`. Open `/en`:
   - Discovery heading reads `13 skills for your team.` (or whatever the
     skills total is after seeding — should match the count of catalog
     skills, not the cross-category total).
   - **Recommended** tab fills ~4 rows on a 3-col desktop (≈13 cards).
     Scan for category spread — should see a mix across systemDesign,
     softDev, testing, network, cloud, docs, data, vcs.
   - **Popular** + **New** tabs unchanged in behavior.
3. Repeat on `/zh` — heading should read "13 个技能，为你的团队而备。"
4. Hit the API directly:
   `GET /api/internal/extensions?category=skills&filter=official` —
   `total` should be 13.
5. Lint + typecheck + tests: `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test`.
   None of the changes touch test fixtures or source TypeScript types, so
   the 242-test suite should stay green.

## Commit shape (per CLAUDE.md §Workflow)

Two commits on the existing `refactor/home-cohesion-pass` branch:

- `feat(seed): promote 11 catalog skills to badge=official`
- `refactor(home): heading says "skills"; total filtered to skills-only`

No agent-attribution trailer.

```
1	// Idempotent catalog seed.
2	//
3	// Safe to run on every deploy. Mirrors scripts/seed-mcp-landscape.ts in
4	// strategy: upserts a fixed inventory of extensions (id prefix `cat-`) plus
5	// the small set of supporting rows it needs (default org, tag vocabulary),
6	// then prunes any catalog-prefixed rows no longer in the inventory. Real
7	// user-published extensions (any non-`cat-` id) are never touched.
8	//
9	// Wired into the Vercel build via `vercel-build` so a freshly deployed
10	// environment carries a populated /extensions catalog.
11	
12	import { and, inArray, notInArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm"
13	import { drizzle, type PostgresJsDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/postgres-js"
14	import postgres from "postgres"
15	
16	import { CATALOG, type CatalogEntry } from "../shared/data/catalog"
17	import * as schema from "../shared/db/schema"
18	import {
19	  extensions,
20	  extensionTags,
21	  organizations,
22	  tags,
23	} from "../shared/db/schema"
24	import { TAG_LABELS } from "../shared/tags"
25	
26	type Db = PostgresJsDatabase<typeof schema>
27	
28	const SYSTEM_ORG_ID = "default"
29	const CATALOG_ID_PREFIX = "cat-"
30	
31	function extensionIdFor(entry: CatalogEntry): string {
32	  return `${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}${entry.slug}`
33	}
34	
35	/**
36	 * Upserts the catalog into `extensions` + `extension_tags`. Re-runnable;
37	 * never destructive to user data. The whole body runs inside one
38	 * transaction so a mid-step failure rolls back cleanly — particularly
39	 * important for step 4, where a crash between the delete and the insert
40	 * would otherwise leave catalog extensions with no tag links.
41	 *
42	 *   1. Ensure SYSTEM_ORG_ID exists (the owner for catalog rows).
43	 *   2. Upsert every tag from TAG_LABELS so referenced ids resolve in prod.
44	 *   3. Upsert each catalog entry by extension id.
45	 *   4. Replace tag links for each upserted extension (delete-then-insert
46	 *      scoped by extensionId — surgical, only the catalog row is touched).
47	 *   5. Prune extensions with id LIKE 'cat-%' not in the current CATALOG.
48	 */
49	export async function seedCatalog(db: Db): Promise<void> {
50	  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
51	    // 1. Owner org — same constant as seed-mcp-landscape; safe to upsert again.
52	    await tx
53	      .insert(organizations)
54	      .values({
55	        id: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
56	        slug: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
57	        name: "Default Organization",
58	        nameZh: "默认组织",
59	      })
60	      .onConflictDoNothing({ target: organizations.id })
61	
62	    // 2. Tag vocabulary. seed.ts seeds these in dev; in prod nothing else
63	    //    does, so we upsert here too. onConflictDoNothing keeps existing
64	    //    rows verbatim in case admin curation has edited a label by hand.
65	    const tagRows = Object.entries(TAG_LABELS).map(([id, label]) => ({
66	      id,
67	      labelEn: label.en,
68	      labelZh: label.zh,
69	    }))
70	    await tx.insert(tags).values(tagRows).onConflictDoNothing()
71	    console.log(`seed-catalog: ensured ${tagRows.length} tags`)
72	
73	    // 3. Extension upserts. Catalog rows are visibility=published with a
74	    //    fixed publishedAt (so re-runs don't shift sort order); ownerOrgId
75	    //    is the SYSTEM_ORG; publisherUserId is null (the schema allows it).
76	    //    deptId stays null — catalog rows are department-agnostic.
77	    const now = new Date()
78	    const extRows = CATALOG.map((entry) => ({
79	      id: extensionIdFor(entry),
80	      slug: entry.slug,
81	      category: entry.category,
82	      scope: entry.scope,
83	      funcCat: entry.funcCat,
84	      subCat: entry.subCat,
85	      l2: entry.l2,
86	      badge: entry.badge ?? null,
87	      publisherUserId: null,
88	      ownerOrgId: SYSTEM_ORG_ID,
89	      deptId: null,
90	      iconEmoji: entry.iconEmoji,
91	      iconColor: entry.iconColor,
92	      visibility: "published" as const,
93	      name: entry.name,
94	      nameZh: entry.nameZh,
95	      tagline: entry.tagline,
96	      taglineZh: entry.taglineZh,
97	      description: entry.description,
98	      descriptionZh: entry.descriptionZh,
99	      downloadsCount: entry.downloadsCount,
100	      starsAvg: entry.starsAvg,
101	      ratingsCount: Math.floor(entry.downloadsCount / 50),
102	      publishedAt: now,
103	    }))
104	
105	    await tx
106	      .insert(extensions)
107	      .values(extRows)
108	      .onConflictDoUpdate({
109	        target: extensions.id,
110	        set: {
111	          slug: sql`excluded.slug`,
112	          category: sql`excluded.category`,
113	          scope: sql`excluded.scope`,
114	          funcCat: sql`excluded.func_cat`,
115	          subCat: sql`excluded.sub_cat`,
116	          l2: sql`excluded.l2`,
117	          badge: sql`excluded.badge`,
118	          ownerOrgId: sql`excluded.owner_org_id`,
119	          iconEmoji: sql`excluded.icon_emoji`,
120	          iconColor: sql`excluded.icon_color`,
121	          visibility: sql`excluded.visibility`,
122	          name: sql`excluded.name`,
123	          nameZh: sql`excluded.name_zh`,
124	          tagline: sql`excluded.tagline`,
125	          taglineZh: sql`excluded.tagline_zh`,
126	          description: sql`excluded.description`,
127	          descriptionZh: sql`excluded.description_zh`,
128	          downloadsCount: sql`excluded.downloads_count`,
129	          starsAvg: sql`excluded.stars_avg`,
130	          ratingsCount: sql`excluded.ratings_count`,
131	          updatedAt: sql`now()`,
132	        },
133	      })
134	    console.log(`seed-catalog: upserted ${extRows.length} extensions`)
135	
136	    // 4. Tag links — replace per-extension to keep edits in sync with the
137	    //    data file. Scoped delete + insert is safe: it never touches other
138	    //    rows. The transaction makes the delete+insert atomic so the table
139	    //    is never observed mid-state.
140	    const catalogExtIds = extRows.map((r) => r.id)
141	    await tx
142	      .delete(extensionTags)
143	      .where(inArray(extensionTags.extensionId, catalogExtIds))
144	    const tagLinkRows = CATALOG.flatMap((entry) =>
145	      entry.tags.map((tagId) => ({
146	        extensionId: extensionIdFor(entry),
147	        tagId,
148	      })),
149	    )
150	    if (tagLinkRows.length > 0) {
151	      await tx
152	        .insert(extensionTags)
153	        .values(tagLinkRows)
154	        .onConflictDoNothing()
155	    }
156	    console.log(`seed-catalog: wired ${tagLinkRows.length} tag links`)
157	
158	    // 5. Prune catalog rows whose slug has been removed from CATALOG. Bound
159	    //    by the `cat-` id prefix so non-catalog extensions are never affected.
160	    const desiredIds = catalogExtIds
161	    const stale = await tx
162	      .delete(extensions)
163	      .where(
164	        and(
165	          sql`${extensions.id} LIKE ${`${CATALOG_ID_PREFIX}%`}`,
166	          desiredIds.length > 0
167	            ? notInArray(extensions.id, desiredIds)
168	            : sql`true`,
169	        ),
170	      )
171	      .returning({ id: extensions.id })
172	    if (stale.length > 0) {
173	      console.log(`seed-catalog: pruned ${stale.length} stale entries (${stale.map((s) => s.id).join(", ")})`)
174	    } else {
175	      console.log(`seed-catalog: no stale entries to prune`)
176	    }
177	  })
178	}
179	
180	// ─── Standalone entry-point: `bun scripts/seed-catalog.ts` ────────────────────
181	async function main() {
182	  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL
183	  if (!url) {
184	    console.error("seed-catalog: DATABASE_URL is not set")
185	    process.exit(1)
186	  }
187	  const client = postgres(url)
188	  const db = drizzle(client, { schema, casing: "snake_case" })
189	
190	  console.log("seed-catalog: starting")
191	  await seedCatalog(db)
192	  console.log("seed-catalog: done")
193	  await client.end()
194	}
195	
196	const isEntry = import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`
197	if (isEntry) {
198	  main()
199	    .then(() => process.exit(0))
200	    .catch((err) => {
201	      console.error("seed-catalog: failed")
202	      console.error(err)
203	      process.exit(1)
204	    })
205	}
206	
```

> AGENT

Found it. The catalog file is the source for the **seed script** — it doesn't change the live DB until that script runs. The good news: the seed correctly upserts `badge` for existing rows (`scripts/seed-catalog.ts:117` sets `badge: sql\`excluded.badge\`` on conflict), so re-running it will flip the 10 entries from no-badge to `official`.

Until that runs, the DB still has the old badge values and the API still returns 3 official skills.

You have two ways forward:

1. **Local dev** — run it yourself in your terminal so you can see the output:
   ```
   ! bun run scripts/seed-catalog.ts
   ```
   (the `!` prefix lets it run with your env loaded; needs `DATABASE_URL` set)

2. **Vercel** — push the branch and deploy; `vercel-build` runs `seed-catalog.ts` automatically (`package.json:9-10`).

I can run option 1 for you if you'd like — it touches your dev database, so I want to confirm before doing it. Want me to?

> DEVELOPER

do we need add or update some tests for this branch?

> AGENT

Honest take: most of the branch is CSS/copy refinement that no test would meaningfully cover, but there's one cheap, high-value test worth adding — a **catalog invariant** asserting that ≥12 catalog skills carry `badge: "official"` spanning multiple `funcCat`s. The badge set is editorial intent that lives in plain TS, and a casual edit could silently drop the Recommended tab back to 3 cards.

The TopBar structural changes (Trending link, More dropdown, Publish icon) are real behavior, but there's no existing `TopBar.nuxt.test.ts` in the repo and the changes are mostly visible-on-load — writing tests now would be net-new infrastructure rather than maintaining an existing pattern. I'd skip.

So my recommendation: **one small test on `shared/data/catalog.ts`**, nothing else. Want me to add it?

A casual badge edit could silently shrink the\n// tab back to a single row; this test makes that change loud.\ndescribe(\"catalog: official-skill curation\", () => {\n  const officialSkills = CATALOG.filter(\n    (e) => e.category === \"skills\" && e.badge === \"official\",\n  )\n\n  it(\"includes at least 12 official skills\", () => {\n    expect(officialSkills.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(12)\n  })\n\n  it(\"spreads official skills across at least 3 functional categories\", () => {\n    const funcCats = new Set(officialSkills.map((e) => e.funcCat))\n    expect(funcCats.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3)\n  })\n})\n"
